\displaystyle\textsf{Err}_{\infty}=\max\Big\{\|\nabla\mathfrak{L}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{\ast}+\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{\ast})\|_{\ell_{\infty}},\min\nolimits_{\|\bm{\mathcal{X}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\infty}\|\nabla\mathfrak{L}(\bm{\mathcal{X}})\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\Big\}=\|\bm{\mathcal{Z}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}

(12.36)

So we have (
12.35
) holds with probability at least
1-0.5\bar{d}^{-2}
from Lemma
13.4
. Taking union bounds and we get both (
12.35
) and (
12.34
) hold with probability at least
1-\bar{d}^{-2}
. And finally applying Theorem
4.1
and Theorem
4.3
gives the desired result.

12.4
Proof of Lemma
5.2

Denote the event
{\cal E}_{1}=\{\|\bm{\mathcal{Z}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq 2\sqrt{m}\sigma_{z}\sqrt{\log(\bar{d})}\}
, then from Lemma
13.4
, we have
{\cal E}_{1}
holds with probability at least
1-2(d^{*})^{-1}
.
Now we set
\tau_{l}=2\sqrt{m}\sigma_{z}\sqrt{\log(\bar{d})}+(d^{*})^{-1/2}\mu_{1}\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}
, then under
{\cal E}_{1}
, we have
\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\tau_{l}
.
From the definition of
\tau_{0}
, we have
|\tau_{0}|\leq|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}|^{(\lfloor pd^{*}-|\Omega^{*}|\rfloor)}\leq\tau_{l}
.
Denote
\Omega_{1}=\{\omega:|[\bm{\mathcal{A}}]_{\omega}|\leq\tau_{0}\}

From the definition of
\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{0}\|_{\rm F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\omega\in\Omega_{1}}[\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}]_{\omega}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{\omega\in\Omega_{1}}[\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}]_{\omega}^{2}+2\sum_{\omega\in\Omega_{1}\cap\Omega^{*}}[\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}]_{\omega}[\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}]_{\omega}

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{\omega\in\Omega_{1}}[\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}]_{\omega}^{2}-4|\Omega^{*}|\tau_{l}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}\|_{\rm F}^{2}-\sum_{\omega\in\Omega_{1}^{c}}[\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}]_{\omega}^{2}-4|\Omega^{*}|\tau_{l}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}\|_{\rm F}^{2}-(pd^{*}+4|\Omega^{*}|)\tau_{l}^{2},

(12.37)

where the penultimate inequality holds since for all
\omega
,
|[\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}]_{\omega}|\leq\tau_{l}
and for all
\omega\in\Omega_{1}
, we have
|[\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}]_{\omega}|\leq|[\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}]_{\omega}|+\tau_{0}\leq 2\tau_{l}
. Now we estimate the lower bound for
\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}\|_{\rm F}^{2}
. Since
\bm{\mathcal{Z}}
has i.i.d. subgaussian entries, we have
\|\bm{\mathcal{Z}}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\geq\frac{1}{2}d^{*}\sigma_{z}^{2}
with probability at least
1-2\exp(-cd^{*})
for some absolute constant
c>0
, and
2\langle\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*},\bm{\mathcal{Z}}\rangle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+2\sigma_{z}^{2}\log(\bar{d})
with probability at least
1-2(d^{*})^{-1}
. Put these altogether, we see

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}\|_{\rm F}^{2}=\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+\|\bm{\mathcal{Z}}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+2\langle\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*},\bm{\mathcal{Z}}\rangle\geq\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\sigma_{z}^{2}d^{*}.

(12.38)

Combine (
12.4
) and (
12.38
), we have

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{0}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\geq\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\sigma_{z}^{2}d^{*}-(pd^{*}+4|\Omega^{*}|)\tau_{l}^{2}.

(12.39)

Therefore with the choice
\tau=10\sqrt{m}\sqrt{\log(\bar{d})}\mu_{1}\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{A}}_{0}\|_{\rm F}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}
, we see that

\tau\geq\tau_{l}
and
\tau_{u}:=10\sqrt{m}\sqrt{\log(\bar{d})}\mu_{1}\tau_{l}\geq\tau
.
With such a choice of
\tau
, since for
\omega\in(\Omega^{*})^{c}
, we have
|[\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}]_{\omega}+[\bm{\mathcal{Z}}]_{\omega}|\leq\tau_{l}\leq\tau
, so we obtain

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{A}}}

\displaystyle={\cal P}_{(\Omega^{*})^{c}}(\bm{\mathcal{A}})+{\cal P}_{\Omega^{*}}(\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{A}}})={\cal P}_{(\Omega^{*})^{c}}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}})+{\cal P}_{\Omega^{*}}(\textsf{Trunc}_{\tau}(\bm{\mathcal{A}}))

\displaystyle=\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}+{\cal P}_{\Omega^{*}}(\textsf{Trunc}_{\tau}(\bm{\mathcal{A}})-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\bm{\mathcal{Z}})

\displaystyle=:\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{Z}}+\mathbb{E},

where
\bm{\mathcal{E}}={\cal P}_{\Omega^{*}}(\textsf{Trunc}_{\tau}(\bm{\mathcal{A}})-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\bm{\mathcal{Z}})

and
the first equality holds since for
\omega\in(\Omega^{*})^{c}
,
|[\bm{\mathcal{A}}]_{\omega}|\leq|[\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}]|_{\omega}+|[\bm{\mathcal{Z}}_{\omega}]|\leq\tau_{u}
. Under event
{\cal E}_{1}
, we have
\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}\|_{\rm F}\leq 2|\Omega^{*}|^{1/2}\tau_{u}
.

Now we use bold-face capital letters as shorthand notation for the unfolding of corresponding calligraphic-font bold-face letters, for example,
\mathbf{T}_{i}^{*}=\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),i\in[m]
. We denote
\bm{\mathcal{X}}=\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{E}}
. We also denote
\mathbf{U}_{i}^{*}
be the top
r_{i}
left singular vectors of
\mathbf{T}_{i}^{*}
,
\mathbf{V}_{i}
be the top
r_{i}
left singular vectors of
\mathbf{X}_{i}
and
\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{i}^{0}
be the top
r_{i}
left singular vectors of
\widetilde{}\mathbf{A}_{i}
.

From Wedin’s sin
\Theta
theorem, we have from condition
(a)
,

\displaystyle d_{c}(\mathbf{U}_{i}^{*},\mathbf{V}_{i})\leq\frac{C|\Omega^{*}|^{1/2}\tau_{u}}{\underline{\lambda}},

(12.40)

where
d_{c}(\mathbf{U},\mathbf{V})=\min_{\mathbf{R}\in\mathbb{O}_{r}}\|\mathbf{U}\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{V}\|
.
Meanwhile, from
\|\mathbf{X}_{i}-\mathbf{T}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm F}=\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}\|_{\rm F}\leq|\Omega^{*}|^{1/2}\tau_{u}
,
we also have
\sigma_{r_{i}}(\mathbf{X}_{i})\geq\frac{3\underline{\lambda}}{4}
,
\sigma_{r_{i}+1}(\mathbf{X}_{i})\leq\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{4}
and
\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\|\leq\frac{5\bar{\lambda}}{4}
.

Since subtracting a multiple of identity matrix does not change the top eigenvectors,
in order to bound the distance
d_{c}(\mathbf{V}_{i},\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{i}^{0})
,
we consider
\|\widetilde{}\mathbf{A}_{i}\widetilde{}\mathbf{A}_{i}^{T}-\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{T}-\sigma_{v}^{2}d_{i}^{-}\mathbf{I}_{d_{i}}\|
, where
\sigma_{v}^{2}
is the variance of the entry of
\bm{\mathcal{Z}}
and
d_{i}^{-}=d^{*}/d_{i}
.
In fact, we have

\widetilde{}\mathbf{A}_{i}\widetilde{}\mathbf{A}_{i}^{T}-\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{T}-\sigma_{v}^{2}d_{i}^{-}\mathbf{I}_{d_{i}}=\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{T}+\mathbf{Z}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{T}+\mathbf{Z}_{i}\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{T}-\sigma_{v}^{2}d_{i}^{-}\mathbf{I}_{d_{i}}.

Now we first consider the operator norm of
\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{T}
under the event
{\cal E}_{1}
. From Talagrand’s concentration inequality, we have

\mathbb{P}\bigg(\big|\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{T}\|-\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{T}\|\big|\leq C_{m}\sqrt{\log(\bar{d})}\sigma_{z}\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\|\cdot t\bigg|{\cal E}_{1}\bigg)\geq 1-2\exp(-ct^{2}).

Since
\mathbb{P}({\cal E}_{1})\geq 1/2
and from
[
Vershynin 2011
, Theorem 1.1]
, we have
\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{T}\||{\cal E}_{1}]\leq 2\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{T}\|\leq C\sqrt{d_{i}}\sigma_{z}\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\|
. Therefore setting
t=\sqrt{\log(\bar{d})}
and the event

{\cal E}_{2}^{i}=\{\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{T}\|\leq C_{m}\sqrt{d_{i}}\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\|\sigma_{z}\},\quad{\cal E}_{2}=\cap_{i=1}^{m}{\cal E}_{2}^{i},

we know that
\mathbb{P}({\cal E}_{2}|{\cal E}_{1})\geq 1-2m\bar{d}^{-1}
and thus
\mathbb{P}({\cal E}_{2})\geq(1-2m\bar{d}^{-1})(1-2(d^{*})^{-1})
.

Now we turn to bounding
\|\mathbf{Z}_{i}\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{T}-d_{i}^{-}\sigma_{z}^{2}\mathbf{I}_{d_{i}}\|
. From
[
Vershynin 2018
, Theorem 4.6.1]
, we have with probability exceeding
1-2\exp(-d_{i})
,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Z}_{i}\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{T}-d_{i}^{-}\sigma_{z}^{2}\mathbf{I}_{d_{i}}\|\leq C(d^{*})^{1/2}\sigma_{z}^{2}.

Denote the event
{\cal E}_{3}^{i}=\{\|\mathbf{Z}_{i}\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{T}-d_{i}^{-}\sigma_{v}^{2}\mathbf{I}_{d_{i}}\|\leq C(d^{*})^{1/2}\sigma_{z}^{2}\}
and
{\cal E}_{3}=\cap_{i=1}^{m}{\cal E}_{3}^{i}
and we have
\mathbb{P}({\cal E}_{3})\geq 1-2\sum_{i=1}^{m}\exp(-d_{i})
.
Therefore under the event
{\cal E}_{2},{\cal E}_{3}
,
and from condition
(b)
,
we have

d_{c}(\mathbf{V}_{i},\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{i}^{0})\leq\frac{C_{m}\sqrt{\bar{d}}\sigma_{z}\bar{\lambda}+C(d^{*})^{1/2}\sigma_{z}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{2}}.

Together with (
12.40
), we have

\displaystyle d_{c}(\mathbf{U}_{i}^{*},\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{i}^{0})\leq\frac{C_{m}\sqrt{\bar{d}}\sigma_{z}\bar{\lambda}+C(d^{*})^{1/2}\sigma_{z}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{2}}+\frac{C|\Omega^{*}|^{1/2}\tau_{u}}{\underline{\lambda}}.

(12.41)

Denote the event

{\cal E}_{4}=\left\{\max_{i=1}^{m}\max_{\|\mathbf{V}_{j}\|\leq 1,j\neq i}\|\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\mathbf{V}_{i+1}\otimes\cdots\otimes\mathbf{V}_{m}\otimes\mathbf{V}_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes\mathbf{V}_{i-1})\|\leq C_{m}(\sqrt{\bar{d}\bar{r}}+\bar{r}^{\frac{m-1}{2}})\sigma_{z}\right\}.

And from
[
Zhang and Xia 2018
, Lemma 5]
, we have
\mathbb{P}({\cal E}_{4})\geq 1-Cm\exp(-c\bar{d})
.
For the following we denote

\displaystyle\mathbf{X}_{1}^{t}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}_{1}^{*}(\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{2}^{t}\otimes\cdots\otimes\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{m}^{t})=\mathbf{T}_{1}^{*}({\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{2}^{*}}\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{2}^{t}\otimes\cdots\otimes{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{m}^{*}}\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{m}^{t})

\displaystyle\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{t}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{Z}_{1}(\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{2}^{t}\otimes\cdots\otimes\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{m}^{t})

\displaystyle\widetilde{}\mathbf{A}_{1}^{t}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{}\mathbf{A}_{1}(\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{2}^{t}\otimes\cdots\otimes\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{m}^{t}),

where
{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}}=\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{T}
.
We shall denote
L_{t}=\max_{i=1}^{m}d_{c}(\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{i}^{t},\mathbf{U}_{i}^{*})
. For the base case, from (
12.41
) and condition
(b)
, we see
L_{0}\leq\frac{1}{2}
.
Now suppose we have
L_{t}\leq\frac{1}{2}
.

From the process of HOOI, we have
\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{t+1}=\textrm{SVD}_{r_{1}}(\widetilde{}\mathbf{A}_{1}(\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{2}^{t}\otimes\cdots\otimes\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{m}^{t}))
.
And thus we obtain

\displaystyle\sigma_{r_{1}}(\mathbf{X}_{1}^{t})

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{r_{1}}(\mathbf{U}_{2}^{*}\otimes\cdots\otimes\mathbf{U}_{m}^{*})\cdot\prod_{i=2}^{m}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{U}_{i}^{*T}\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{i}^{t})

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{r_{1}}(\mathbf{U}_{2}^{*}\otimes\cdots\otimes\mathbf{U}_{m}^{*})(1-L_{t}^{2})^{(m-1)/2}

\displaystyle\geq c_{m}(1-L_{t})^{2}\underline{\lambda},

(12.42)

for some small constant
c_{m}>0
depending only on
m
, and the last inequality holds since
1-L_{t}^{2}\geq\frac{3}{4}
. We bound
\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{t}\|
under the event
{\cal E}_{4}
.

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{t}\|

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}(\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{2}^{t}\otimes\cdots\otimes\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{m}^{t})\|

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\big(({\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{2}^{*}}+{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{2}^{*}}^{\perp})\otimes\cdots\otimes({\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{m}^{*}}+{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{m}^{*}}^{\perp})\big)(\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{2}^{t}\otimes\cdots\otimes\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{m}^{t})\|

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}[(\bar{d})^{1/2}+\bar{r}^{(m-1)/2}]\sigma_{z}+C_{m}[(\bar{d}\bar{r})^{1/2}+\bar{r}^{(m-1)/2}]\sigma_{z}L_{t},

(12.43)

where the last inequality holds since
{\cal E}_{4}
holds and
\|\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{i}^{tT}\mathbf{U}_{i\perp}^{*}\|\leq L_{t}
.
Now since
\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{t+1}
is the top
r_{1}
left singular vectors of
\widetilde{}\mathbf{A}_{1}^{t}
and
\mathbf{U}_{1}
is the top
r_{1}
left singular vectors of
\mathbf{X}_{1}^{t}
, from Wedin’s sin
\Theta
Theorem, we have

\displaystyle d_{c}(\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{t+1},\mathbf{U}_{1})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\|\widetilde{}\mathbf{A}_{1}^{t}-\mathbf{X}_{1}^{t}\|}{\underline{\lambda}}\leq\frac{C(\|\mathbf{E}_{1}\|_{\rm F}+\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{t}\|)}{\underline{\lambda}}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{Z1t}}{\leq}\frac{C|\Omega^{*}|^{1/2}\tau_{u}+C_{m}[(\bar{d})^{1/2}+\bar{r}^{(m-1)/2}]\sigma_{z}+C_{m}[(\bar{d}\bar{r})^{1/2}+\bar{r}^{(m-1)/2}]\sigma_{z}L_{t}}{\underline{\lambda}}.

The derivation for
d_{c}(\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{i}^{t+1},\mathbf{U}_{i})
when
i\geq 2
is similar to this case and hence

\displaystyle L_{t+1}\leq\frac{C|\Omega^{*}|^{1/2}\tau_{u}+C_{m}[(\bar{d})^{1/2}+\bar{r}^{(m-1)/2}]\sigma_{z}}{\underline{\lambda}}+\frac{C_{m}[(\bar{d}\bar{r})^{1/2}+\bar{r}^{(m-1)/2}]\sigma_{z}}{\underline{\lambda}}L_{t}.

From condition
(b)
, we have
C_{m}[(\bar{d}\bar{r})^{1/2}+\bar{r}^{(m-1)/2}]\sigma_{z}/\underline{\lambda}\leq 1/2
, so the above inequality implies

\displaystyle L_{t_{\max}}\leq(\frac{1}{2})^{t_{\max}}\cdot L_{0}+\frac{C|\Omega^{*}|^{1/2}\tau_{u}+C_{m}[(\bar{d})^{1/2}+\bar{r}^{(m-1)/2}]\sigma_{z}}{\underline{\lambda}}.

If we choose
t_{\max}\geq(C_{m}\log(\bar{d}\kappa_{0})\vee 1)
, then

\displaystyle L_{t_{\max}}\leq\frac{C|\Omega^{*}|^{1/2}\tau_{u}}{\underline{\lambda}}+\frac{C_{m}[(\bar{d})^{1/2}+\bar{r}^{(m-1)/2}]\sigma_{z}}{\underline{\lambda}}.

(12.44)

Set the event
{\cal E}_{5}=\{\|\bm{\mathcal{Z}}\times_{i=1}^{m}{\cal P}_{\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{i}}\|_{\rm F}\leq C(r^{*}+\sum_{i=1}^{m}d_{i}r_{i})\sigma_{z}^{2}\}
.
Then from
[
Zhang and Xia 2018
, Lemma 5]
,
\mathbb{P}({\cal E}_{5})\geq 1-\exp(-C\bar{d}\bar{r})
.
And we also consider
\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\times_{i}\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{i\perp}^{T}\|_{\rm F}
, we consider
i=1
for simplicity.

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\times_{1}\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{1\perp}^{T}\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\|\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{1\perp}^{T}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\|{\cal P}_{\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{1\perp}}\mathbf{T}_{1}^{*}(\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{2}^{t_{\max}-1}\otimes\cdots\otimes\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{m}^{t_{\max}-1})\|_{\rm F}\cdot\prod_{i=2}^{m}\sigma_{\min}^{-1}(\mathbf{U}_{i}^{*T}\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{i}^{t_{\max}-1})

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}(\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}\|_{\rm F}+\sqrt{r_{1}}\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{t_{\max}-1}\|)

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}|\Omega^{*}|^{1/2}\tau_{u}+C_{m}(\sqrt{d_{1}r_{1}}+\sqrt{r^{*}})\sigma_{z},

(12.45)

where the second inequality holds from
[
Zhang and Xia 2018
, Lemma 6]
and the last inequality holds from (
12.4
).

Now we are in the right position to bound
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}
under
{\cal E}_{5}
.

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\|\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{A}}}\times_{i=1}^{m}{\cal P}_{\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{i}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\|(\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{A}}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\times_{i=1}^{m}{\cal P}_{\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{i}}\|_{\rm F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\times_{i=1}^{m}{\cal P}_{\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{i}}\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{E}}\|_{\rm F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{Z}}\times_{i=1}^{m}{\cal P}_{\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{i}}\|_{\rm F}+\sum_{i=1}^{m}\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\times_{i}\widehat{}\mathbf{U}_{i\perp}^{T}\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}|\Omega^{*}|^{1/2}\tau_{u}+C_{m}(\sqrt{r^{*}}+\sqrt{\bar{d}\bar{r}})\sigma_{z},

(12.46)

where the last inequality follows from (
12.4
).
Finally applying Lemma
13.6
and we get
\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}
is
(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{2}
-incoherent and
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq 2\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}
. Therefore from condition
(a),(b)
in Lemma
5.2
, the initialization condition
(a)
in Theorem
5.1
holds.

12.5
Proof of Lemma
5.3

For each
j\in[m]
and
i\in[d_{j}]
, we have

\|\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}{\cal M}_{j}(\bm{\mathcal{S}}_{\alpha})\|_{\ell_{0}}=\sum\nolimits_{\omega:\omega_{j}=i}\mathbbm{1}\big(|[\bm{\mathcal{Z}}]_{\omega}|>\alpha\sigma_{z}\big)=\sum\nolimits_{\omega:\omega_{j}=i}[\bm{\mathcal{Y}}]_{\omega}

where
\bm{\mathcal{Y}}\in\{0,1\}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
having
i.i.d.
Bernoulli entries and
q:=\mathbb{P}([\bm{\mathcal{Y}}]_{\omega}=1)=\mathbb{P}(|[\bm{\mathcal{Z}}]_{\omega}|>\alpha\sigma_{z})\leq\alpha^{-\theta}
.

Denote
X_{ij}=\sum\nolimits_{\omega:\omega_{j}=i}[\bm{\mathcal{Y}}]_{\omega}
. By Chernoff bound, if
d_{j}^{-}q\geq 3\log(m\bar{d}^{3})
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(X_{ij}-d_{j}^{-}q\geq d_{j}^{-}q\Big)\leq\exp\big\{-d_{j}^{-1}q/3\big\}\leq(m\bar{d}^{3})^{-1}

implying that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(\bigcap\nolimits_{i,j}\big\{X_{ij}\leq 2d_{j}^{-}q\big\}\Big)\geq 1-m\bar{d}(m\bar{d}^{3})^{-1}=1-\bar{d}^{-2}.

(12.47)

On the other hand, if
d_{j}^{-}q\leq 3\log(m\bar{d}^{3})
, by Chernoff bound, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(X_{ij}\geq 10\log(m\bar{d}^{3})\Big)\leq(m\bar{d}^{3})^{-1}

implying that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Big(\bigcap\nolimits_{i,j}\big\{X_{ij}\leq 10\log(m\bar{d}^{3})\big\}\Big)\geq 1-m\bar{d}(m\bar{d}^{3})^{-1}=1-\bar{d}^{-2}.

(12.48)

Putting (
12.47
) and (
12.48
), since
q\leq\alpha^{-\theta}
, we get

\mathbb{P}\Big(\bigcap\nolimits_{i,j}\Big\{X_{ij}\leq\max\big\{10\log(m\bar{d}^{3}),2d_{j}^{-}\alpha^{-\theta}\big\}\Big\}\Big)\geq 1-\bar{d}^{-2},

which completes the proof.

12.6
Proof of Theorem
5.4

Conditioned on
\mathfrak{E}_{1}
defined in Lemma
5.3
, Theorem
5.4
is a special case of Theorem
5.1
. Indeed, in Theorem
5.1
, we replace
\sigma_{z}
with
\alpha\sigma_{z}
, and
|\Omega^{\ast}|\log\bar{d}
with
\alpha^{\prime}d^{\ast}\asymp\bar{d}\log(m\bar{d})
, then we get Theorem
5.4
.

12.7
Proof of Lemma
5.5

From the choice of
\alpha
in Theorem
5.4
, we see that the sparsity of
\bm{\mathcal{S}}_{\alpha}
is bounded by
\alpha^{\prime}\asymp\frac{\bar{d}}{d^{*}}\log(m\bar{d}^{3})
. Therefore the condition
(a)
in Lemma
5.2
is satisfied. Now applying Lemma
5.2
and we get the desired result.

12.8
Proof of Lemma
5.6

From Lemma
13.6
, we have
\textsf{Trim}_{\eta,\mathbf{r}}(\bm{\mathcal{W}})
is
2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0}
-incoherent. Now for all
j\in[m]
,

\|\mathcal{M}_{j}(\mathscr{H}^{{\rm HO}}_{\mathbf{r}}(\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{W}}}))\|\leq\|\mathcal{M}_{j}(\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{W}}})\|\leq\|\mathcal{M}_{j}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|+\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\frac{9}{8}\bar{\lambda}.

So we conclude

\|\textsf{Trim}_{\eta,\mathbf{r}}(\bm{\mathcal{W}})\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\frac{9}{8}\bar{\lambda}\prod_{i=1}^{m}(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})\sqrt{\frac{r_{j}}{d_{j}}}\leq(9\zeta/16)\cdot(\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{m}.

where the last inequality follows from the upper bound for
\bar{\lambda}
. This finishes the proof of the lemma.

12.9
Proof of Theorem
5.7

From the choice of
\zeta^{\prime}
and Lemma
5.6
, we know Assumption
2
and
3
hold with parameters
b_{l,\zeta^{\prime}}
and
b_{u,\zeta^{\prime}}
with respect to the set
\mathbb{B}_{2}^{\ast}=\mathbb{B}_{\infty}^{\ast}=\{\bm{\mathcal{T}}+\bm{\mathcal{S}}:\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}+\bm{\mathcal{S}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\zeta^{\prime},\bm{\mathcal{T}}\in\mathbb{M}_{\mathbf{r}},\bm{\mathcal{S}}\in\mathbb{S}_{\gamma\alpha}\}
.
Now the proof follows the proof of Theorem
4.1
with slight modification. Since we can now guarantee in each iteration
\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}+\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}_{l}\in\mathbb{B}_{2}^{\ast}=\mathbb{B}_{\infty}^{\ast}
from Lemma
5.6
and the choice of
\textsf{k}_{\textsf{pr}}
, we can use Assumption
3
instead of Assumption
2
when estimating the low rank part.
So we only need to estimate
\textsf{Err}_{\infty}
and
\textsf{Err}_{2\mathbf{r}}
. From (
5.8
), we have
\textsf{Err}_{\infty}\leq L_{\zeta}
. Now we estimate
\textsf{Err}_{2\mathbf{r}}
. In fact, from the definition of
\textsf{Err}_{2\mathbf{r}}
, we have

\textsf{Err}_{2\mathbf{r}}=\sup_{\bm{\mathcal{M}}\in\mathbb{M}_{2\mathbf{r}},\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|_{\rm F}\leq 1}\langle\nabla\mathfrak{L}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}),\bm{\mathcal{M}}\rangle.

Since for all
\omega\in[d_{1}]\times\ldots\times[d_{m}]
, we have
[\nabla\mathfrak{L}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})]_{\omega}
is bounded random variable with the upper bound given by
L_{\zeta}
. So apply Lemma
13.3
, we have
\textsf{Err}_{2\mathbf{r}}\leq CL_{\zeta}\cdot(\bar{d}\bar{r}+r^{*})^{1/2}
with with probability at least
1-\bar{d}^{-2}
. Now we plug in the bounds for
\textsf{Err}_{\infty}
and
\textsf{Err}_{2\mathbf{r}}
to Theorem
4.1
and we get the first part of the theorem. For the
\ell_{\infty}
bound, we apply Theorem
4.3
and Lemma
13.7
. And we finish the proof of the theorem.

12.10
Proof of Lemma
5.8

Algorithm 7

Initialization for binary tensor

Let
\mathbf{A}=\bm{\mathcal{A}}^{\langle m_{0}\rangle}:=\textsf{reshape}(\bm{\mathcal{A}},[d_{1}\ldots d_{m_{0}},d_{m_{0}+1}\ldots d_{m}])
with
m_{0}=\lfloor\frac{m}{2}\rfloor
and let
\widehat{}\mathbf{M}
be the minimizer to (
12.49
).

\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}=\textsf{reshape}(\widehat{}\mathbf{M},[d_{1},\ldots,d_{m}])
.

\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}=\textsf{Trim}_{\eta,\mathbf{r}}(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}})
with
\eta=16\mu_{1}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\|_{\rm F}/(7\sqrt{d^{*}})
.

Output:
\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}
.

We first introduce some notations. Let
m_{0}=\lfloor\frac{m}{2}\rfloor
, and denote
\mathbf{T}^{*}=(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m_{0}\rangle}
,
\mathbf{S}^{*}=(\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})^{\langle m_{0}\rangle}
and
\mathbf{A}=\bm{\mathcal{A}}^{\langle m_{0}\rangle}
, then
\mathbf{T}^{*},\mathbf{S}^{*},\mathbf{A}
are matrices of size
d_{1}\ldots d_{m_{0}}\times d_{m_{0}+1}\ldots d_{m}=:d_{1}^{*}\times d_{2}^{*}
. Since
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{\ast}
admits the decomposition
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{\ast}=\bm{\mathcal{C}}^{\ast}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{U}_{1}^{\ast},\cdots,\mathbf{U}_{m}^{\ast}\rrbracket
, we have
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}=(\mathbf{U}_{m_{0}}\otimes\cdots\otimes\mathbf{U}_{1})\bm{\mathcal{C}}^{\langle m_{0}\rangle}(\mathbf{U}_{m}\otimes\cdots\otimes\mathbf{U}_{m_{0}+1})^{T}
and hence the rank of
\mathbf{T}^{*}
is
r=\min\{r_{1}\cdots r_{m_{0}},r_{m_{0}+1}\cdots r_{m}\}
. We denote
\mathbf{M}=\mathbf{T}^{*}+\mathbf{S}^{*}
.

Under Assumption
5
, we have
\|\mathbf{T}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}},\|\mathbf{S}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\frac{\zeta}{2}
and thus
\|\mathbf{M}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\zeta
. Now we bound the nuclear norm of
\mathbf{M}
. Using triangle inequality and we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{M}\|_{\ast}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{T}^{*}\|_{\ast}+\|\mathbf{S}^{*}\|_{\ast}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\zeta}{2}(rd^{*})^{1/2}+\frac{\zeta}{2}|\Omega^{*}|^{1/2}\min(d_{1}^{*},d_{2}^{*})^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\big(\frac{\zeta}{2}+\frac{\zeta}{2}\cdot\frac{\min(d_{1}^{*},d_{2}^{*})^{1/2}}{(rd^{*})^{1/2}}|\Omega^{*}|^{1/2}\big)(rd^{*})^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\zeta(rd^{*})^{1/2},

where the last inequality holds since condition
(a)
holds.
Now with a little bit abuse of notation, we consider the following convex program,

\displaystyle\min\mathfrak{L}(\mathbf{X})=-\langle\mathbf{A},\log(p(X))\rangle-\langle 1-\mathbf{A},\log(1-p(\mathbf{X}))\rangle,\text{~s.t.~}\|\mathbf{X}\|_{\ast}\leq\zeta\sqrt{d^{*}r}\text{~and~}\|\mathbf{X}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\zeta,

(12.49)

where the notation
1-\mathbf{A}
is the entrywise subtraction, and
p(\mathbf{X})
is applying
p
entrywisely to
\mathbf{X}
.
Denote
\widehat{}\mathbf{M}
be the minimizer to (
12.49
) and apply the Theorem 1 in
Davenport et al. 2014
with the sample size
d^{*}
and we get with probability at least
1-\frac{C}{d_{1}^{*}+d_{2}^{*}}
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{}\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\leq C_{\zeta}[r(d_{1}^{*}+d_{2}^{*})d^{*}]^{1/2}

with
C_{\zeta}=C\cdot\zeta L_{\zeta}\beta_{\zeta}
and
\beta_{\zeta}=\sup_{|x|\leq\zeta}\frac{p(x)(1-p(x))}{(p^{\prime}(x))^{2}}
.

Now we reshape
\widehat{}\mathbf{M}
back to a tensor, and denote
\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}=\textsf{reshape}(\widehat{}\mathbf{M},[d_{1},\ldots,d_{m}])
. Since
reshape
keeps the Frobenius norm unchanged, we have

\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}=\|\widehat{}\mathbf{T}-\mathbf{T}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\|\widehat{}\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}\|_{\rm F}+\|\mathbf{S}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq C_{\zeta}^{1/2}[r(d_{1}^{*}+d_{2}^{*})d^{*}]^{1/4}+|\Omega^{*}|^{1/2}\frac{\zeta}{2}.

Finally we output
\bm{\mathcal{T}}_{0}=\textsf{Trim}_{\eta,\mathbf{r}}(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}})
with
\eta=16\mu_{1}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\|_{\rm F}/(7\sqrt{d^{*}})
, and from Lemma
5.6
and Lemma
13.6
, since condition
(b)
and
(c)
hold, we get

(1)~\mu(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0})\leq 2\kappa_{0}\mu_{1};~(2)~\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq 2\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F};~(3)~\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq C_{m}(\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{m}\frac{\sqrt{r^{*}}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\bar{\lambda}.

And together with the upper bound for
\underline{\lambda}
in Assumption
5
, the initialization condition in Theorem
5.7
is satisfied.

12.11
Proof of Theorem
5.9

The proof of this theorem is similar to that of Theorem
5.7
. From the choice of
\zeta^{\prime}
and Lemma
5.6
, we know Assumption
2
and
3
hold with parameters
b_{l,\zeta^{\prime}}=e^{-\zeta^{\prime}}
and
b_{u,\zeta^{\prime}}=e^{\zeta^{\prime}}
with respect to the set
\mathbb{B}_{2}^{\ast}=\mathbb{B}_{\infty}^{\ast}=\{\bm{\mathcal{T}}+\bm{\mathcal{S}}:\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}+\bm{\mathcal{S}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\zeta^{\prime},\bm{\mathcal{T}}\in\mathbb{M}_{\mathbf{r}},\bm{\mathcal{S}}\in\mathbb{S}_{\gamma\alpha}\}
.
Now the proof follows the proof of Theorem
4.1
with slight modification. Since we can now guarantee in each iteration
\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}+\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}_{l}\in\mathbb{B}_{2}^{\ast}=\mathbb{B}_{\infty}^{\ast}
from Lemma
5.6
and the choice of
\textsf{k}_{\textsf{pr}}
, we can use Assumption
3
instead of Assumption
2
when estimating the low rank part.
So we only need to estimate
\textsf{Err}_{\infty}
and
\textsf{Err}_{2\mathbf{r}}
. From (
5.8
), we have
\textsf{Err}_{\infty}\leq\|\nabla\mathfrak{L}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})\|_{\ell_{\infty}}
. Simple calculation shows

\nabla\mathfrak{L}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})=-\frac{1}{I}\bm{\mathcal{Y}}+\exp(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}),

and notice using a union bound and Poisson’s tail bound, when
I\geq Ce^{\zeta}\log(d^{*})
, we have with probability exceeding
1-\frac{1}{d^{*}}
,
\|\bm{\mathcal{Y}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq 10Ie^{\zeta}
. Therefore we have
\textsf{Err}_{\infty}\leq 11e^{\zeta}
.

The estimation for
\textsf{Err}_{2\mathbf{r}}
is given in Theorem 4.3
Han et al. 2020
, which states

\textsf{Err}_{2\mathbf{r}}\leq C\sqrt{\frac{r^{*}+m\bar{d}\bar{r}}{I/e^{\zeta}}}

with probability exceeding
1-\frac{1}{d^{*}}
.

Now we plug in the bounds for
\textsf{Err}_{\infty}
and
\textsf{Err}_{2\mathbf{r}}
to Theorem
4.1
and we get the first part of the theorem. For the
\ell_{\infty}
bound, we apply Theorem
4.3
and Lemma
13.7
. And we finish the proof of the theorem.

12.12
Proof of Lemma
5.10

With slight modification of the proof of Theorem 4.3 in
Han et al. 2020
, we have

\|\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq C\sqrt{\frac{e^{\zeta}}{I}}(\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sqrt{d_{i}r_{i}}+\sqrt{d^{-}_{i}r_{i}})+\|\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}

under the condition
I\geq Ce^{\zeta}\bar{d}
with probability exceeding
1-1/d^{*}
. Therefore since we assume
I\geq C_{1}\sum_{i=1}^{m}(d_{i}r_{i}+d_{i}^{-}r_{i})\bar{r}\underline{\lambda}^{-2}
and
|\Omega^{*}|\leq C\zeta^{-2}\underline{\lambda}^{2}\bar{r}^{-1}
, we have
\|\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq c_{1,m}\underline{\lambda}\cdot\min\big\{\delta^{2}\bar{r}^{-1/2},(\kappa_{0}^{2m}\bar{r}^{1/2})^{-1}\big\}\leq\underline{\lambda}/8
. Now we apply Lemma
5.6
and Lemma
13.6
we see

(1)~\mu(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0})\leq 2\kappa_{0}\mu_{1};~(2)~\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq 2\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F};~(3)~\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq C_{m}(\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{m}\frac{\sqrt{r^{*}}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\bar{\lambda}.

From Assumption
6
, we see the initialization requirements in
5.9
is satisfied.

12.13
Proof of Theorem
6.1

We use induction to prove this theorem.

Step 0: Base case.

From the initialization, we have
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq c_{1,m}\delta\bar{r}^{-1/2}\cdot\underline{\lambda}
.

Step 1: Estimating
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}
.

We prove this case assuming

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq c_{1,m}\delta\bar{r}^{-1/2}\cdot\underline{\lambda}.

(12.50)

We point out that this also implies
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq c_{1,m}b_{l}b_{u}^{-1}\bar{r}^{-1/2}\cdot\underline{\lambda}
since
\delta\lesssim b_{l}^{2}b_{u}^{-2}
.
In order to use Lemma
13.2
, we need to derive an upper bound for
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\beta{\cal P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm F}
.

Step 1.1: Estimating
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\beta{\cal P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm F}
.

For arbitrary
1\geq\delta>0
, we have,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\beta{\cal P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\leq(1+\delta/2)\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\beta{\cal P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{G}}^{*})\|_{\rm F}^{2}+(1+2/\delta)\beta^{2}\|{\cal P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}\bm{\mathcal{G}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}

(12.51)

Now we consider the bound for
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\beta{\cal P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{G}}^{*})\|_{\rm F}^{2}
.

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\beta\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{G}}^{*})\|_{\rm F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}-2\beta\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{G}}^{*})\rangle+\beta^{2}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{G}}^{*})\|_{\rm F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\beta^{2}b_{u}^{2})\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}-2\beta\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{G}}^{*})\rangle

(12.52)

where the last inequality holds from the Assumption
2
since
\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}\in\mathbb{B}_{2}^{*}
from (
12.50
).
Also,

\displaystyle\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*},\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{G}}^{*})\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*},\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{G}}^{*}\rangle-\langle{\cal P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}^{\perp}(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{G}}^{*}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq b_{l}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}-\frac{C_{1,m}b_{u}}{\underline{\lambda}}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{3}

(12.53)

where the last inequality is from Assumption
2
, Lemma
13.1
and Cauchy-Schwartz inequality and
C_{1,m}=2^{m}-1
. Together with (
12.13
) and (
12.13
), and since we have
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\frac{0.1b_{l}}{2b_{u}C_{1,m}}\cdot\underline{\lambda}
, we get,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\beta\mathcal{P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}(\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{G}}^{*})\|_{\rm F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-2\beta b_{l}+\beta^{2}b_{u}^{2})\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+\frac{2\beta C_{1,m}b_{u}}{\underline{\lambda}}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq(1-1.9\beta b_{l}+\beta^{2}b_{u}^{2})\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}.

(12.54)

Since we have
0.75b_{l}b_{u}^{-1}\geq\delta^{1/2}
, if we choose
\beta\in[0.4b_{l}b_{u}^{-2},1.5b_{l}b_{u}^{-2}]
, we have
1-1.9\beta b_{l}+\beta^{2}b_{u}^{2}\leq 1-\delta
.

So from (
12.51
) and (
12.13
), we get

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\beta{\cal P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\leq(1+\frac{\delta}{2})(1-\delta)\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+(1+\frac{2}{\delta})\textsf{Err}_{2\mathbf{r}}^{2}

(12.55)

where in the inequality we use the definition of
\textsf{Err}_{2\mathbf{r}}
and that
\beta\leq 1
. Now from the upper bound for
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}
and the signal-to-noise ratio, we verified that
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\beta{\cal P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm F}\leq\underline{\lambda}/8
and thus
\sigma_{\max}(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\beta{\cal P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l})\leq\underline{\lambda}/8
.

Step 1.2: Estimating
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}
.

Now that we verified the condition of Lemma
13.2
, from the Algorithm
5
, we have,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\leq\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\beta{\cal P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+C_{m}\frac{\sqrt{\bar{r}}}{\underline{\lambda}}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\beta{\cal P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm F}^{3}

(12.56)

where
C_{m}>0
is the constant depending only on
m
as in Lemma
13.2
. From (
12.55
) and the assumption that
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\lesssim_{m}\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{\bar{r}}}\cdot\underline{\lambda}
and
\textsf{Err}_{2\mathbf{r}}\lesssim_{m}\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{\bar{r}}}\cdot\underline{\lambda}
, we get

\displaystyle C_{m}\frac{\sqrt{\bar{r}}}{\underline{\lambda}}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\beta{\cal P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm F}\leq\frac{\delta}{4}

(12.57)

From (
12.56
), (
12.55
) and (
12.57
), we get

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\leq(1+\frac{\delta}{4})\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\beta{\cal P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}\bm{\mathcal{G}}_{l}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\leq(1-\delta^{2})\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+\frac{4}{\delta}\textsf{Err}_{2\mathbf{r}}^{2}

(12.58)

Together with the assumption
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\lesssim_{m}\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{\bar{r}}}\cdot\underline{\lambda}
and
\textsf{Err}_{2\mathbf{r}}\lesssim_{m}\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{\bar{r}}}\cdot\underline{\lambda}
, we get

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq c_{1,m}\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{\bar{r}}}\cdot\underline{\lambda},

(12.59)

which completes the induction and completes the proof.

13
Technical Lemmas

Lemma 13.1
.

Suppose
\mathbb{T}_{l}
is the tangent space at the point
\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}
, then we have

\displaystyle\|{\cal P}_{\mathbb{T}_{l}}^{\perp}\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\frac{2^{m}-1}{\underline{\lambda}}\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}\|_{\rm F}^{2}.

Proof.

See (
Cai et al. 2020
, Lemma 5.2).
∎

Lemma 13.2
.

Let
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}=\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\cdot(\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*},\ldots,\mathbf{V}_{m}^{*})
be the tensor with Tucker rank
\mathbf{r}=(r_{1},\ldots,r_{m})
. Let
\bm{\mathcal{D}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}\times\ldots\times d_{m}}
be a perturbation tensor such that
\underline{\lambda}\geq 8\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\mathcal{D}})
, where
\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\mathcal{D}})=\max_{i=1}^{m}\|\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{D}})\|
. Then we have

\|\mathscr{H}^{{\rm HO}}_{\mathbf{r}}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{D}})-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm F}+C_{m}\frac{\sqrt{\bar{r}}\|\bm{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm F}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}}

where
C_{m}>0
is an absolute constant depending only on
m
.

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we only prove the Lemma in the case
m=3
.
First notice that

\mathscr{H}^{{\rm HO}}_{\mathbf{r}}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{D}})=(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{D}})\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{1}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{2}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{3}}\rrbracket,

where
\mathbf{U}_{i}
are leading
r_{i}
left singular vectors of
\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}+\bm{\mathcal{D}})
and
{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{i}}=\mathbf{U}_{i}\mathbf{U}_{i}^{\top}
.

First from (
Xia 2019
, Theorem 1), we have for all
i\in[m]

{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{i}}-{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}}=\mathcal{S}_{i,1}+\sum_{j\geq 2}\mathcal{S}_{i,j},

where
\mathcal{S}_{i,j}=\mathcal{S}_{\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),j}(\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{D}}))
and specially
\mathcal{S}_{i,1}=(\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\top})^{\dagger}(\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{D}}))^{\top}{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}}^{\perp}+{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}}^{\perp}\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{D}})(\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}))^{\dagger}
. The explicit form of
S_{i,j}
can be found in
[
Xia 2019
, Theorem 1]
.
Here, we denote
\mathbf{A}^{\dagger}
the pseudo-inverse of
\mathbf{A}
, i.e.,
\mathbf{A}^{\dagger}=\mathbf{R}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{-1}\mathbf{L}^{\top}
if
\mathbf{A}
has a thin-SVD as
\mathbf{A}=\mathbf{L}\mathbf{\Sigma}\mathbf{R}^{\top}
. With a little abuse of notations, we write
(\mathbf{A}^{\dagger})^{k}=\mathbf{R}\mathbf{\Sigma}^{-k}\mathbf{L}^{\top}
for any positive integer
k\geq 1
.

For the sake of brevity, we denote
\mathbf{S}_{i}=\sum_{j\geq 1}{\cal S}_{i,j}
. By the definition of
{\cal S}_{i,j}
, we have the bound
\|\mathcal{S}_{i,j}\|\leq\left(\frac{4\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\mathcal{D}})}{\underline{\lambda}}\right)^{j}
. We get the upper bound for
\|\mathbf{S}_{i}\|
as follows,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{S}_{i}\|=\|\sum_{j\geq 1}\mathcal{S}_{i,j}\|\leq\frac{4{\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\mathcal{D}})}}{\underline{\lambda}-4\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\mathcal{D}})}\leq\frac{8\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\mathcal{D}})}{\underline{\lambda}}

(13.1)

So we have,

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot

\displaystyle\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{1}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{2}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{3}}\rrbracket=\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}}+\mathbf{S}_{1},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\ast}}+\mathbf{S}_{2},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}+\mathbf{S}_{3}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket

(13.2)

\displaystyle+\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket+\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},\mathbf{S}_{2},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket+\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},\mathbf{S}_{3}\rrbracket

\displaystyle+\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},\mathbf{S}_{2},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket+\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},\mathbf{S}_{2},\mathbf{S}_{3}\rrbracket+\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},\mathbf{S}_{3}\rrbracket

\displaystyle~~~~+\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},\mathbf{S}_{2},\mathbf{S}_{3}\rrbracket

(13.3)

We now bound each of
\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},\mathbf{S}_{2},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket\|_{\rm F}
,
\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},\mathbf{S}_{2},\mathbf{S}_{3}\rrbracket\|_{\rm F}
and
\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},\mathbf{S}_{3}\rrbracket\|_{\rm F}
. Without loss of generality, we only prove the bound of the first term.

\displaystyle{\cal M}_{1}\big(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},\mathbf{S}_{2},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket\big)=\mathbf{S}_{1}\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\left({\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{S}_{2}\right)^{\top}

(13.4)

Write

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{1}\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})

\displaystyle=\left({\cal S}_{1,1}+\sum_{j\geq 2}{\cal S}_{1,j}\right)\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})

\displaystyle={\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}}^{\perp}\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\mathcal{D}})\left(\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\right)^{\dagger}\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})+\sum_{j\geq 2}{\cal S}_{1,j}\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})

\displaystyle={\cal M}_{1}\big(\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}}^{\perp},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket\big)+\sum_{j\geq 2}{\cal S}_{1,j}\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})

(13.5)

where we used the fact
{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}}^{\perp}{\cal M}_{1}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})={\bf 0}
.

Thus we obtain an upper bound for
\|\mathbf{S}_{1}\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|
as follows

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{S}_{1}\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|\leq\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\mathcal{D}})+\underline{\lambda}\sum_{j\geq 2}\left(\frac{4\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\mathcal{D}})}{\underline{\lambda}}\right)^{j}\leq 4\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\mathcal{D}}),

(13.6)

where the first inequality is due to the explicit form of
{\cal S}_{1,j}
. See
[
Xia 2019
, Theorem 1]
.

So from (
13.4
) and (
13.6
), we get

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},\mathbf{S}_{2},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{S}_{1}\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm F}\cdot\|{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{S}_{2}\|\leq C_{1}\sqrt{\bar{r}}\frac{\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\mathcal{D}})^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}}

(13.7)

where
C_{1}>0
is an absolute constant.

Now we consider the linear terms
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket
,
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},\mathbf{S}_{2},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket
and
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},\mathbf{S}_{3}\rrbracket
. Clearly, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{1}\left(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{S}_{1}\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{2}\left(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},\mathbf{S}_{2},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{S}_{2}\mathcal{M}_{2}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{\ast})

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{3}\left(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},\mathbf{S}_{3}\rrbracket\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{S}_{3}\mathcal{M}_{3}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{\ast}),

(13.8)

whose explicit representations are already studied in eq. (
13
). As a result, we can write

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},

\displaystyle{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket+\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},\mathbf{S}_{2},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket+\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},\mathbf{S}_{3}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}}^{\perp},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket+\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}}^{\perp},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket+\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}^{\perp}\rrbracket

\displaystyle+\sum_{j\geq 2}\Big(\mathcal{M}_{1}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\cdot\llbracket{\cal S}_{1,j},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\ast}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\ast}}\rrbracket+\mathcal{M}_{2}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\ast}},{\cal S}_{2,j},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\ast}}\rrbracket+\mathcal{M}_{3}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\ast}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\ast}},{\cal S}_{3,j}\rrbracket\Big).

(13.9)

Now we bound
\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{1}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{2}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{3}}\rrbracket
as follows

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket

\displaystyle{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{1}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{2}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{3}}\rrbracket=\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}}+\mathbf{S}_{1},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}}+\mathbf{S}_{2},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}+\mathbf{S}_{3}\rrbracket

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket

\displaystyle+\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket+\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},\mathbf{S}_{2},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket+\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},\mathbf{S}_{3}\rrbracket

\displaystyle+\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},\mathbf{S}_{2},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket+\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}},\mathbf{S}_{2},\mathbf{S}_{3}\rrbracket+\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},\mathbf{S}_{3}\rrbracket

\displaystyle~~~~+\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},\mathbf{S}_{2},\mathbf{S}_{3}\rrbracket

(13.10)

Similarly as proving the bound (
13.7
), we can show

\displaystyle\max\left\{\|\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket\|_{\rm F},\|\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},\mathbf{S}_{2},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket\|_{\rm F},\|\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{S}_{1},\mathbf{S}_{2},\mathbf{S}_{3}\rrbracket\|_{\rm F}\right\}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{\bar{r}}\frac{\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\mathcal{D}})^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}}

(13.11)

where
C_{1}>0
is an absolute constant.

Finally, by (
13
), (
13.7
), (
13
) and (
13.11
), we have

\displaystyle\big\|(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}

\displaystyle+\bm{\mathcal{D}})\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{1}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{2}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{U}_{3}}\rrbracket-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\big\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}}^{\perp},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket+\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}^{*}_{1}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}}^{\perp},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket+\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}^{*}_{1}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}^{\perp}\rrbracket+\bm{\mathcal{D}}\cdot\llbracket{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}^{*}_{1}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{2}^{*}},{\cal P}_{\mathbf{V}_{3}^{*}}\rrbracket\big\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle~~~~+C_{1}\frac{\sqrt{\bar{r}}\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\mathcal{D}})^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm F}+C_{2}\frac{\sqrt{\bar{r}}\sigma_{\max}(\bm{\mathcal{D}})^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}}

(13.12)

where
C_{1},C_{2}>0
are absolute constants (
C_{2,m}=16m+2^{m+1}
in the case of general
m
). This finishes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Lemma 13.3
.

Assume all the entries of
\bm{\mathcal{Z}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}\times\ldots\times d_{m}}
are independent mean-zero random variables with bounded Orlicz-
\psi_{2}
norm:

\|[\bm{\mathcal{Z}}]_{\omega}\|_{\psi_{2}}=\sup_{q\geq 1}(\mathbb{E}|[\bm{\mathcal{Z}}]_{\omega}|^{q})^{1/q}/q^{1/2}\leq\sigma_{z}

Then there exists some constants
C_{m},c_{m}>0
depending only on
m
such that

\sup_{\bm{\mathcal{M}}\in\mathbb{M}_{2\mathbf{r}},\|\bm{\mathcal{M}}\|_{F}\leq 1}\langle\bm{\mathcal{Z}},\bm{\mathcal{M}}\rangle\leq C_{m}\sigma_{z}\left(r^{*}+\sum_{i=1}^{m}d_{i}r_{i}\right)^{1/2}

with probability at least
1-\exp(-c_{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}d_{i}r_{i})
, where
r^{*}=r_{1}\ldots r_{m}
.

Proof.

See the proof of (
Han et al. 2020
, Lemma D.5).
∎

Lemma 13.4

(Maximum of sub-Gaussian)
.

Let
Z_{1},\ldots,Z_{N}
be
N
random variables such that
\mathbb{E}\exp\{tZ_{i}\}\leq\exp\{t^{2}\sigma_{z}^{2}/2\}
for all
i\in[N]
. Then

\mathbb{P}(\max_{1\leq i\leq N}|Z_{i}|>t)\leq 2N\exp(-\frac{t^{2}}{2\sigma_{z}^{2}}).

Proof.

The claim follows from the following two facts:

\mathbb{P}(\max_{1\leq i\leq N}Z_{i}>t)\leq\mathbb{P}(\cup_{1\leq i\leq N}\{Z_{i}>t\})\leq N\mathbb{P}(Z_{i}>t)\leq N\exp(-\frac{t^{2}}{2\sigma_{z}^{2}}),

and

\max_{1\leq i\leq N}|Z_{i}|=\max_{1\leq i\leq 2N}Z_{i}

with
Z_{N+i}=-Z_{i}
for
i\in[N]
.
∎

Lemma 13.5

(Spikiness implies incoherence)
.

Let
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\in\mathbb{M}_{\mathbf{r}}
satisfies Assumption
1
with parameter
\mu_{1}
. Then we have:

\mu(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\leq\mu_{1}\kappa_{0}.

where
\mu(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})
is the incoherence parameter of
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}
and
\kappa_{0}
is the condition number of
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}
.

Proof.

Denote
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}=\bm{\mathcal{C}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket\mathbf{U}_{1},\ldots,\mathbf{U}_{m}\rrbracket
.
Now we check the incoherence condition of
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}
. For all
i\in[d_{j}]
and
j\in[m]
,

\|\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{j}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\ell_{2}}=\|\mathbf{e}_{i}\mathbf{U}_{j}\mathcal{M}_{j}(\bm{\mathcal{C}}^{*})\|_{\ell_{2}}\geq\|\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{j}\|_{\ell_{2}}\cdot\underline{\lambda}\geq\|\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{j}\|_{\ell_{2}}\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}}{\sqrt{r_{j}}\kappa_{0}}.

On the other hand, we have

\|\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{j}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq\sqrt{d_{j}^{-}}\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\mu_{1}\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{j}}},

where the last inequality is due to the spikiness condition
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}
satisfies. Together with these two inequalities, we have

\|\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{j}\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{r_{j}}{d_{j}}}\mu_{1}\kappa_{0}.

And this finishes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Lemma 13.6
.

Let
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\in\mathbb{M}_{\mathbf{r}}
satisfies Assumption
1
with parameter
\mu_{1}
. Suppose that
\bm{\mathcal{W}}
satisfies
\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{8}
, then we have
\textsf{Trim}_{\zeta,\mathbf{r}}(\bm{\mathcal{W}})
is
(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{2}
-incoherent if we choose
\zeta=\frac{16}{7}\mu_{1}\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\rm F}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}
. Also, it satisfies

\|\textsf{Trim}_{\zeta,\mathbf{r}}(\bm{\mathcal{W}})-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}+\frac{C_{m}\sqrt{\bar{r}}\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}},

where
C_{m}>0
depends only on
m
.

Proof.

Notice
\textsf{Trim}_{\zeta,\mathbf{r}}(\bm{\mathcal{W}})=\mathscr{H}^{{\rm HO}}_{\mathbf{r}}(\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{W}}})
, where
\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{W}}}
is the entrywise truncation of
\bm{\mathcal{W}}
with the thresholding
\zeta/2
. To check the incoherence of
\mathscr{H}^{{\rm HO}}_{\mathbf{r}}(\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{W}}})
, denote
\widetilde{}\mathbf{U}_{j}
the top-
r_{j}
left singular vectors of
{\cal M}_{j}(\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{W}}})
, and
\widetilde{}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{j}
the
r_{j}\times r_{j}
diagonal matrix containing the top-
r_{j}
singular values of
{\cal M}_{j}(\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{W}}})
. Then, there exist a
\widetilde{}\mathbf{V}_{j}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{j}^{-}\times r_{j}}
satisfying
\widetilde{}\mathbf{V}_{j}^{\top}\widetilde{}\mathbf{V}_{j}=\mathbf{I}_{r_{j}}
such that

\widetilde{}\mathbf{U}_{j}\widetilde{}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{j}={\cal M}_{j}(\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{W}}})\widetilde{}\mathbf{V}_{j}.

Now we can also bound the
\ell_{\infty}
-norm of
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}
:

\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\mu_{1}\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\leq\mu_{1}\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\rm F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\bm{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\rm F}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\leq\mu_{1}\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\rm F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}/8}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}.

This together with the definition of
\zeta
, we have:

\mu_{1}\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\leq 8/7\cdot\mu_{1}\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\rm F}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}=\zeta/2.

And thus
\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\zeta/2
.
Then for all
i\in[d_{j}]
,

\|\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{}\mathbf{U}_{j}\|_{\ell_{2}}=\|\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{j}(\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{W}}})\widetilde{}\mathbf{V}_{j}\widetilde{}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{j}^{-1}\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq\frac{\|\mathbf{e}_{i}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{j}(\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{W}}})\|_{\ell_{2}}}{\lambda_{r_{j}}(\widetilde{}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{j})}\leq\frac{\zeta/2\cdot(d_{j}^{-})^{1/2}}{7/8\cdot\lambda_{r_{j}}(\mathcal{M}_{j}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}))}.

where the last inequality is due to
\|\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{W}}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\underline{\lambda}/8
since
\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\zeta/2
and
\|\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{W}}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\zeta/2
. Meanwhile,

\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\sqrt{r_{j}}\kappa_{0}\lambda_{r_{j}}(\mathcal{M}_{j}(\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})).

There for the
\zeta=\frac{16}{7}\mu_{1}\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\rm F}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}
, we have for all
j\in[m]

\max_{i\in[d_{j}]}\|\mathbf{e}_{i}\widetilde{}\mathbf{U}_{j}\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq\frac{64}{49}\mu_{1}\kappa_{0}\frac{\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}+\underline{\lambda}/8}{\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}}\sqrt{\frac{r_{j}}{d_{j}}}\leq 2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{j}}{d_{j}}}.

where the second last inequality is from
\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\rm F}\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}+\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}
and the last inequality is from
\|\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\geq\underline{\lambda}
.

The second claim follows from the fact that
\|\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{W}}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\underline{\lambda}/8
, and from Lemma
13.2
,

\displaystyle\|\textsf{Trim}_{\zeta,\mathbf{r}}(\bm{\mathcal{W}})-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}=\|\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{W}}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{W}}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}+C_{m}\frac{\sqrt{\bar{r}}\|\widetilde{\bm{\mathcal{W}}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}+C_{m}\frac{\sqrt{\bar{r}}\|\bm{\mathcal{W}}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}}

This finishes the proof of the lemma.
∎

We introduce some notations for the following lemmas. Denote by
\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}=\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}\cdot(\mathbf{U}_{1},\ldots,\mathbf{U}_{m})
,
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}=\bm{\mathcal{C}}^{*}\cdot(\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*},\ldots,\mathbf{U}_{m}^{*})
.

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{i}=\arg\min_{\mathbf{R}\in\mathbb{O}_{r_{i}}}\|\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{U}_{i}^{*}\mathbf{R}\|_{\rm F},i\in[m]

(13.13)

If we let
\mathbf{U}_{i}^{*T}\mathbf{U}_{i}=\mathbf{L}_{i}\mathbf{S}_{i}\mathbf{W}_{i}^{\top}
be the SVD of
\mathbf{U}_{i}^{*T}\mathbf{U}_{i}
, then the closed form of
\mathbf{R}_{i}
is given by
\mathbf{R}_{i}=\mathbf{L}_{i}\mathbf{W}_{i}^{\top}
. And we rewrite

\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}=\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\cdot(\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*},\cdots,\mathbf{V}_{m}^{*})

where
\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}=\bm{\mathcal{C}}^{*}\cdot(\mathbf{R}_{1}^{\top},\cdots,\mathbf{R}_{m}^{\top})
and
\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}=\mathbf{U}_{i}^{*}\mathbf{R}_{i},i\in[m]
. So
\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}
is also
\mu_{0}
-incoherent.

Lemma 13.7

(Entry-wise estimation of
|[\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}]_{\omega}|
)
.

Suppose
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}
satisfies Assumption
1
. Under the assumptions that
\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}
is
(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{2}
-incoherent and
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{16m\bar{r}^{1/2}\kappa_{0}}
, then we have

|[\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}]_{\omega}|^{2}\leq C_{m}\bar{r}^{m}\underline{d}^{-(m-1)}(\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{4m}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2},

where
C_{m}=2^{4m+1}(m+1)
.

Proof.

First we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})\cdot(\mathbf{U}_{1},\cdots,\mathbf{U}_{m})+\sum_{i=1}^{m}\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\cdot(\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*},\ldots,\mathbf{V}_{i-1}^{*},\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*},\mathbf{U}_{i+1},\ldots,\mathbf{U}_{m})

(13.14)

From Lemma
13.5
, we get
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}
is
\mu_{1}^{2}\kappa_{0}^{2}
-incoherent. So we have for all
\omega=(\omega_{1},\ldots,\omega_{m})\in[d_{1}]\times\ldots\times[d_{m}]

\displaystyle|[\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}]_{\omega}|

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\prod_{i=1}^{m}\|(\mathbf{U}_{i})_{\omega_{i}:}\|+\sum_{i=1}^{m}\|\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\|(\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*})_{\omega_{i}:}\|\prod_{k=1}^{i-1}\|(\mathbf{V}_{k}^{*})_{\omega_{k}:}\|\prod_{k=i+1}^{m}\|(\mathbf{U}_{k})_{\omega_{k}:}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{r^{*}}{d^{*}}}(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{2m}\|\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}+(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{2m-2}\sqrt{\frac{\bar{r}^{m-1}}{\underline{d}^{m-1}}}\|\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\|(\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*})_{\omega_{i}:}\|

where
r^{*}=\prod_{i=1}^{m}r_{i},d^{*}=\prod_{i=1}^{m}d_{i}
and
\bar{r}=\max_{i=1}^{m}r_{i},\underline{d}=\min_{i=1}^{m}d_{i}
.
From AG–GM inequality, we have

\displaystyle|[\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}]_{\omega}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(m+1)(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{4m}\frac{r^{*}}{d^{*}}\|\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+(m+1)(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{4m-4}\frac{\bar{r}^{m-1}}{\underline{d}^{m-1}}\|\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\|(\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*})_{\omega_{i}:}\|^{2}

(13.15)

\displaystyle\leq(m+1)\bar{r}^{m}\underline{d}^{-(m-1)}(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{4m}\left(\|\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+\underline{\lambda}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\|\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2(m+1)\bar{r}^{m}\underline{d}^{-(m-1)}(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{4m}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}

where the last inequality is from Lemma
13.9
, and this finishes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Lemma 13.8

(Estimation of
\|{\cal P}_{\Omega}(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm F}^{2}
)
.

Let
\Omega
be the
\alpha
-fraction set. Suppose
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}
satisfies Assumption
1
. Under the assumptions that
\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}
is
(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{2}
-incoherent and
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{16m\bar{r}^{1/2}\kappa_{0}}
, we have

\|{\cal P}_{\Omega}(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm F}^{2}\leq C_{m}(\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{4m}\bar{r}^{m}\alpha\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2},

where
C_{m}=2^{4m+1}(m+1)
.

Proof.

First from (
13.15
) in Lemma
13.7
, we have

|[\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}]_{\omega}|^{2}\leq(m+1)(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{4m}\frac{r^{*}}{d^{*}}\|\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+(m+1)(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{4m-4}\frac{\bar{r}^{m-1}}{\underline{d}^{m-1}}\|\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\|(\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*})_{\omega_{i}:}\|^{2}.

Since
\Omega
is an
\alpha
-fraction set, we have

\displaystyle\|{\cal P}_{\Omega}(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\omega\in\Omega}[\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}]_{\omega}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(m+1)(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{4m}\alpha r^{*}\|\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+(m+1)(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{4m-4}\alpha\bar{r}^{m-1}\|\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\|\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(m+1)(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{4m}\alpha r^{*}\|\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+(m+1)(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{4m-4}\alpha\bar{r}^{m}\overline{\lambda}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\|\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(m+1)(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{4m}\bar{r}^{m}\alpha\left(\|\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+\underline{\lambda}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\|\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\right)

(13.16)

Now we invoke Lemma
13.9
, and we get

\|{\cal P}_{\Omega}(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm F}^{2}\leq 2(m+1)(2\mu_{1}\kappa_{0})^{4m}\bar{r}^{m}\alpha\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2},

which finishes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Lemma 13.9

(Estimation of
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}
)
.

Let
\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}=\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}\cdot(\mathbf{U}_{1},\cdots,\mathbf{U}_{m})
be the
l
-th step value in Algorithm
2
and let
\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}=\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\cdot(\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*},\cdots,\mathbf{V}_{m}^{*})
. Suppose
\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}
satisfies
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{16m\bar{r}^{1/2}\kappa_{0}}
. Then we have the following estimation for
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}
:

\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\geq 0.5\|\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+0.5\underline{\lambda}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\|\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}.

Proof.

First we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})\cdot(\mathbf{U}_{1},\cdots,\mathbf{U}_{m})+\sum_{i=1}^{m}\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\cdot(\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*},\ldots,\mathbf{V}_{i-1}^{*},\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*},\mathbf{U}_{i+1},\ldots,\mathbf{U}_{m})

(13.17)

Notice that we have

\displaystyle\|\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\cdot(\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*},\ldots,\mathbf{V}_{i-1}^{*},\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*},\mathbf{U}_{i+1},\ldots,\mathbf{U}_{m})\|_{\rm F}^{2}=\|(\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*})\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})\|_{\rm F}^{2}

(13.18)

Denote
\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}=\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\cdot(\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*},\ldots,\mathbf{V}_{i-1}^{*},\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*},\mathbf{U}_{i+1},\ldots,\mathbf{U}_{m})
, then we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{m}\|(\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*})\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})\|_{\rm F}^{2}+2\sum_{i<j}\langle\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{j}\rangle+2\sum_{i=1}^{m}\langle(\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})\cdot(\mathbf{U}_{1},\cdots,\mathbf{U}_{m}),\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\|\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{m}\underline{\lambda}^{2}\|\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+2\sum_{i<j}\langle\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{j}\rangle+2\sum_{i=1}^{m}\langle(\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})\cdot(\mathbf{U}_{1},\cdots,\mathbf{U}_{m}),\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\rangle

Notice that
\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})=(\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*})\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})\big(\mathbf{U}_{m}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{i+1}\otimes\mathbf{V}_{i-1}\otimes\mathbf{V}_{1}\big)^{\top}
. So we have the estimation of
|\langle(\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})\cdot(\mathbf{U}_{1},\cdots,\mathbf{U}_{m}),\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\rangle|
is as follows:

\displaystyle|\langle(\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})\cdot(\mathbf{U}_{1},\cdots,\mathbf{U}_{m}),\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\rangle|

\displaystyle=|\langle\mathcal{M}_{i}\big((\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})\cdot(\mathbf{U}_{1},\cdots,\mathbf{U}_{m})\big),\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i})\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\|(\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*})^{\top}\mathbf{U}_{i}\|\|\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\|\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\bar{r}}\bar{\lambda}\|\mathbf{U}_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*})\|_{\rm F}\|\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}

(13.20)

Now we estimate
\|\mathbf{U}_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*})\|_{\rm F}
by plugging in the closed form of
\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}
as in (
13.13
)

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{U}_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*})\|_{\rm F}=\|\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{S}_{i}\|_{\rm F}\leq\|\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{S}_{i}^{2}\|_{\rm F}=\|\mathbf{U}_{i\perp}^{*T}\mathbf{U}_{i}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\leq\|\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{U}_{i}^{*}\mathbf{R}_{i}\|_{\rm F}^{2}

(13.21)

From Wedin’ sin
\Theta
Theorem, we have for
i\in[m]

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\|\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{U}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}}{\underline{\lambda}-\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|}\leq\frac{2\sqrt{2}\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}}{\underline{\lambda}}\leq\frac{1}{4m\bar{r}^{1/2}\kappa_{0}}

(13.22)

where the second last inequality is from
\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|\leq{\underline{\lambda}/2}
and the last inequality is from

\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\leq\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{16m\bar{r}^{1/2}\kappa_{0}}
. Then from (
13
) and (
13.22
), we have

\displaystyle|\langle(\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})\cdot(\mathbf{U}_{1},\cdots,\mathbf{U}_{m}),\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\rangle|\leq\frac{1}{8m^{2}}\|\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\underline{\lambda}^{2}\|\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}

(13.23)

The estimation of
|\langle\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{j}\rangle|(i<j)
is as follows. From
(
13.22
)
, we have

\displaystyle|\langle\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{i},\bm{\mathcal{X}}_{j}\rangle|

\displaystyle=|\langle\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})\mathbf{M}_{i,j},(\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*})^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}\mathcal{M}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*})\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\lambda}\|\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\|\mathbf{M}_{i,j}\|\|(\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*})^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\lambda}\|\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\|(\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*})^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\|(\mathbf{U}_{j}-\mathbf{V}_{j}^{*})^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{j}^{*}\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sqrt{\bar{r}}\bar{\lambda}^{2}\|\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\|\mathbf{U}_{j}-\mathbf{V}_{j}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\frac{1}{16m^{2}}\underline{\lambda}^{2}\|\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm F}\|\mathbf{U}_{j}-\mathbf{V}_{j}^{*}\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{32m^{2}}\underline{\lambda}^{2}\|\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+\frac{1}{32m^{2}}\underline{\lambda}^{2}\|\mathbf{U}_{j}-\mathbf{V}_{j}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}

(13.24)

where
\mathbf{M}_{i,j}=\mathbf{I}\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathbf{I}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{j}^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{j}-\mathbf{V}_{j}^{*})\otimes\mathbf{U}_{j-1}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{j-1}^{*}\otimes\ldots\mathbf{U}_{i+1}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{i+1}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{I}\otimes\ldots\otimes\mathbf{I}
,
(a)
holds because of (
13.21
),
(b)
holds because of (
13.22
).

As a result of (
13
), (
13.23
) and (
13
), we have

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}\geq 0.5\|\bm{\mathcal{C}}_{l}-\bm{\mathcal{S}}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}+0.5\underline{\lambda}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{m}\|\mathbf{U}_{i}-\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm F}^{2}

which finishes the proof of the lemma.
∎
